KA JINGLAMPHRANG

Naduh ba sdang haduh kaba kut kane ka kitab, ka mynsiem 
ka shlei da ka jmgsngewnguh kaba la dei ban ong shi hajar 
nguh la ki nongthoh kot Khasi baroh, bad kyrpang la kito kiba 
la thoh naduh ki por shuwa ba ngin loh 1a ka Jinglaitluid 
(Independence) La ban jer ban niewtang lut ym lah, hynrei ka 
jingkynmaw 1a ki te ka sah.

U Nongthoh u ai-nguh la U Nongthaw ba U la sumar bad 
ri la u, bad pynlah ruh ban la shem bad kiba bun ki Nongthohkot 
kiba mynta kim don shuh hapdengjong ngi U la sngewbha-bok 
shikatdei eh, ba la um lohi shuh 1a ki tymmen mynhyndai kum 
u Parad Rabon, Radhon Sing Berry, Nissor Sing, Morkha Joseph, 
Babu Jcebon Roy, Amjad Ah, Dr John Roberts, pynban u la loh 
lad ban tip pateng shaphang jong kine ki Nongthohkot na ki 
tymmen kiba la ithuh bha bad iohi la ki ha ka doh ha ka snam 
mynba ki dang don ki dang sah ha kane ka pyrthci Na kitei 
ki daw, u Nongthoh u sngewkitram namar kane ka jingiohpateng 
bad u sngew ba ka dei ka kainram ba la kumno kumno dei ban 
ai pateng pat ha ki Longdien la kiei-kiei kiba kordor eh, ba la 
loh-pateng na ki Longshuwa-Manshuwa kum ka nongtymmen 
(heritage) Ki matti u Barim, ki long ka Nongtymmen (heritage) 
kaba koidor katta-katta Uba tip bad fiiewkor la ki, u tip la ka 
non grim, u suh lined u skhern, u roi u pa bad u scisoh ruh ha 
ka jingun

U Nongthoh u la ju ong teng ba kaba tip ei-ei shaphang u 
Nongthohkot bad ka pateng kaba u u im f ka tarap shibun ban 
shemphang la ka jingthoh nc ka kot jong u

Khnang ban ym buh 1a ki Longdien ha ka shaiong, u 
Nongthoh u sngowdei ban kren shai ba um kam ba kme ki 
jingthoh ki long kiba thik bad janai, hynrei u dawa tang ba u 
la pyrshang shitom ban leh la kaba bha tam, katba lah synei 
ban kem 1a ki jingmut ki ktien bad ki kyntien, la ka ngoh ka 
lan, khamtam jong ki juk bad ki por kiba ym lah iohi shuh U 
Nongthoh u 1m jingmut kyndiang ba la jan ka bymlah ban long 
ban ai jmgbatai ka ban pynhun la baroh, namar ym lah hi ban 
khlup lut naduh-haduh

U Nongthoh, katba lah, u la pyrshang ban da dok la ka 
man ka pylla khnang ban ym lah ^hiliang ne khun-ranab.

‘Book on something about something’ released by Shri R T. 
Rymbai (IAS rtd ) on 27-5-96 , that it was wrong to

underestimate small things, as these small things are like 
drops of water which go to make a big river or ocean Shri 
R T Rymbai interpreted some things as small things which 
constitute the totality of life

Apphira 
28 5 96

‘To ngin la wad shuh la ki symboh pyrkhat’ ong u Bah C 
Wolflang, Director N C E R T , ha kaba kren kum u 
Chairman ha ka jingialang ban pyllait paitbah la ka kot 
Katto-Katne Shaphang Ki Katto-Katne ba la thoh da u 
Bah W R Laitflang “Ka kot u Bah Laitflang kan nang 
pynroi bad pynnewspah shuh-shuh la ka thoh ka pule u 
Khasi khamtam la kito kiba angnud ban tip shuh-shuh 
shaphang ka kolshor jong ka jaitbynriew

Mawphor

28-5-96

One of the glaring gaps m the study of the tribes in North 
East India, is the lack of authentic information written by 
the indigenous people themselves The bulk of literature 
about the culture, religion, history and socio-economic life 
of the people is fairly recent and that too, it is written by

observers, rather than participants, of a particular culture 
Needless to say, their ignorance of the language of the 
community they are supposed to study is a hindrance in 
their research Thus much of the literature especially those 
dealing with the finer nuances of culture and religion, is 
at best, partially true

Ma Wil Laitflang’s book, Katto-Katne Shaphang Ki Katto- 
Katne is a timely documentation of various types of 
informations, timely because it fills the existing gap and 
because it puts on written record those informations which 
would otherwise die out with the passing away of older 
people The hundred and four pages are a wealth of 
information, a little bit of almost every aspect of Khasi life, 
from language to culture, from religion to the environment 
The book is the result of painstaking collection of information 
over several years of an enquiring and disciplined mind

Personally I have gleaned much information regarding (i) 
language and lexicology, in expressions like ‘ bam kwai ha 
duwar u Blei\ ‘ mm wai\ ‘ bam t>mai\ ‘ u Pyr sa-Sepsngi\

‘ shongkha pynjot \ (11) cultural practices like *ki nongmm 
waC \ pynryngkang par , l kaba suit ban , (in) religious rituals 
like ‘ Porn blang\ l ka rukom tyngkai met iap\ (iv) 
informations about local history like ‘Mano batih la ka 
Nan Palok\ ‘Jaiaw Laitdom\ (v) information regarding 
flora and fauna like ‘ki jhur kiba sdang da ka ktien ja\ *u 
dieng sohplian , (vi) Personalities like l (J Liar Sordat 
Mawdern\ 'll Duwati Sing Syiem Sohra\ ‘ U Kin Doloi 
Shangpung *

I am sure that readers seeking specific information will 
find it in this book To make it easier for such readers 
some arrangements under subject-wise headings can be 
considered Ma Will, I hope, will not stop at the first volume, 
but God willing, it will continue in many more volumes 
In the present and future genei ations of enculturation and 
acculturation, the wisdom of the ancient past is a diamond 
to be treasured

Juanita Wai 
Professor and Head 
Department of Linguistics, N E H U.

Ha kaba thoh 1a kane ka thup, nga la ong tcng la ki 
paralok, ba ka long sriem kumba shu shim jynrat la ki tiar ki 
tar na phyllaw ne mynshem bad lum noh la ki hapoh mg, tang 
kat ban lait na u slap ka Iyer Kawei pat ruh, nga syier ba loh 
u Por uni ap bad um ai lad Shaphang ka rukom spel dak bad 
jingpymasoh ktien, te nga la shu thoh katba lam ka kti, nga 
da kyrmen ruh ba kito kiba la loh jingpyntbit ha kane ka 
phang, kin sa pynryntih bad buh belt la ka rukom spel dak, ka 
rukom pyniasoh-ktien bad kiwei-kiwei Kane ka long ka kam 
kaba radbah, bad nga ngeit kan urlong da ka jingiatrei lang ki 
nongthohkot, ki nongpathai khubor, ki nonghikai skul bad baroh 
ki para ri

Nga ainguh la u Trai kynrad uba thaw uba buh, namarba 
u dang lsynei ban ai lad bad ai por, namar khlem kata, bi)ai 
kiei-kiei baroh, lada don sap makna, lada don sbai ruh, khlem 
ka por shaei phin tare, shaei phin tda Kiba bun kiei-kiei kim 
da jwat eh lada ka shu kut tang ha ka phylliew-jingmut ne 
pashat pngmut, h>nrei ynda poi ka khep ban batai, te bunsien 
sngew ba jngan bad hateng-hateng jnang ruh

jingsngewnguh 1a ka jmgkyrshan kaba radbah kaba ka rympei

la ki lok ki jor baroh, kiba la leit noh bad kiba dang sah namar

kine

BAN SHU lAPYNNOH LEM

Da ka jingsngewkmen bad jingsngewnguh kaba khraw, 
nga la loh ban pule la ka Manuscript jong ka kot 1 Ma W R 
Laitflang kaba 1 la jer kyrteng. Ki Syrwet Ka Ktien Khasi Ka 
dei ka jmgpyrshang dei por haba ngi peit la ka jmgdawa ban 
pynroi pynnewspah la ka thup-kot halor ka ktien Khasi ha 
kaba sdang jong ka spah-snem kaba arphew-wei Hangne ngam 
shym thmu ban ai la ka bishar-kynthoh (Review) lane ka mait 
phana (Analysis) halor kane ka jingtrei jong 1 Ma Wil. Kaba 
nga kwah ban ai hangne ka long tang khyndiat 1 jmgkynthoh 
halor ki phang kiba 1 la jied ban tai ha kane ka kot jong 1

Ha “ka jinglamphrang”, 1 Nongthoh 1 la maitphang lypa 1a 
kawei na ki thong long kane ka jingtrei jong 1 haba 1 ong ba 1 
“la pyrshang ruh ban da pynbiang matang ban pyniahap dur 
lahap sur kat kum ki jingkylla na ka por sha ka por” Hangne 
ngi shem ba 1 la pyrshang ban tih tynrai 1a ki jingmut barun 
jong ki kyntien ha ka ktien Khasi bad ban pym de kumno ki 
la k>lla dur ha ka rukom pyndonkam la ki ha kme ki por kiba 
mynta

I Nongthoh 1 la phiah la kane ka kot jong 1 ha ki phra tylli 
ki lynnong Ka lynnong kaba nyngkong ka long halor ka Pakhet- 
ktien (Elision) ha ka ktien Khasi Wat la ka lynnong ka long 
kaba lyngkot, pynban 1 Nongthoh 1 la lalam jingmut la ka 
bynta kaba donkam ha ki dur-kyntien jong ka ktien Khasi Ha 
kane ka phang, kumba 1 la kdew lynti, ka long ka shrahbah la 
ki ‘newwad bniah (research scholars) ban wad ban tih kham 
janai la ka Ka lynnong kaba bud pat i la kdew la ka jingiar 
jingiylliew ka kyntien “Khublei” bad ruh halor ka rukom 
pyndonkam 1a ka kyntien ‘ia’ Ha ka lynnong kaba lai 1 Nongthoh 
1 pynpaw 1a ka jmgsngewkhia halor ka jingbympatbeit ryntih 
ka rukom spel dak wat hadien shispah hynriew phew snem ba 
la sdang ka thoh ka pule Khasi Nangta 1 Nongthoh i la jied 
ban batai la ki kyntien kiba long ka dohnud jong ki poim u Soso 
Tham ha Ki Sngi Barim U Hynniew Trep bad Ka Duitara 
Ksiar Ka lynnong kaba san kaba i la khot kyllum “Ki Phalang 
bad ki Pruid” ka kynthup ha ka 1a ki jingbatai halor ki kyntien 
madan (Slangs) ki kyntien shlem (Coined Words), ki kyntien 
ngen bad pynngen (euphemism) bad kiwei de. Ka jingtrei shitom

xi

jong 1 Nongthoh ha ka ban sain thymmai m kiba bun ki kyntien 
kan long ka spah bah na ka bynta ka thup kyntien jong ka 
ktien Khasi Kane ka jingtrei ka la dei ban ktik jmgmut la ki 
nongwad bmah (Researcher) ban nt kham jylliew halor kiba 
kum kine ki jait kyntien Ha kajuh ka lynnong 1 Nongthoh 1 
la kdew la ki lad ban pynriewspah la ka ktien Khasi da kaba 
kdup lem la ki kyntien na ki ktien-tnat (Dialects) kiba kyrhai 
byllai sawdong u pud u sam jong ka Ri Khasi-Pnar

Ka lynnong kaba hynriew ka dei ka jingtrei kaba bor-bah 
bad kaba 1 Nongthoh da kaba suk eh 1 la lah ban pynlong kawei 
ka kot kaba pura Ka kynthup ha ka haduh 91 tylli ki sla ha 
kiba la pynrung la ki kyntien Khasi ryngkat bad ki jmgbatai 
halor jong ki Da kaba lum ba lang 1a ki symboh kyntien bad 
da kaba tai la ka jingmut jong ki ki la pyniar pynriewspah ia 
ka thup-ktien bad ruh la pynneh pynsuh ia ki kyntien kiba la 
sdang ban jah lngaid noh kunito

Ka Lynnong kaba khatduh ka don pat la ka jong ka dor 
namar hangne 1 Ma Wil 1 la lum la lang bad batai de ja ki ‘tien- 
kynnoh jong ka ktien Khasi

Ha baroh ar tylli kitei ki lynnong kiba khatduh, 1 Nongthoh 
1 la lum bun bah ki kvntien ryngkat bad ki jingmut ba la 
pynkit ha ki ban pynbit pynbiang la ka jmgdonkam ha ka ktien 
Khasi katkum ka jmgdawa jong ka por Kum ban shu sot 
nuksa hangne, i lashim la ka kyntien ‘langai' ban la ryngkat 
bad ka kyntien English ‘destiny’ bad ia ka kyntien ‘ka tiphi-ka 
nanghi’ pat na ka bynta ka kyntien English ‘instinct’ Lada yn 
pdiang da uba bun uba lang la kine ki kyntien, tc la nang 
pynriewspah shuh shuh ia u sup u thiar jong ka ktien Khasi

Ki ‘riewrangbah ki khraw-pyrkhat kum 1 Ma Wil, i Ma 
IM. Simon, i Ma H Bareh Ngapkynta bad kiwei de ki dei ki 
nongthoh kiba la lohi la kham slem ia ka sngi u bnai ka pyrthei 
Khasi Ki la shem la mad kham bun la ki jmgjia ha ka jingtyllun 
ka shalyntem ka por Kumta ka jingtip kaba lar kaba jylliew 
kaba ki don ka paw pyrthei lyngba ki jingtiei ba kordor bad ba 
shongsbai kiba ki la pynmih ha ka dur ki kot bad kiwei de ki 
jmgthoh Ka jaitbynnew ka dei ban sngew donbok bad ban 
sngewnguh ia ki na ka bynta ki jingtrei shitom ban lum ban 
lang bad ban ai bynta lem ha ki longdien jong ki la ka jingtip 
baroh kaba ki don

Xll

Khublei shibun, Ma Wil U Blei un kyrkhu, un ai buit ai 
bor ba phin nang lah ban sei shuh sa kiwei de ki kot kum u 
synniang jong phi sha la ka n bad ka jaitbynriew

KA PAKHET-KTIEN

Kawei na ki rukom kren ne pyndonkam ha ka ktien Khasi, 
ka long ka bynta kaba ngi lah ban jer kum ‘Ka Pakhet-ktien’ 
Kane ka rukom ka la pynmih ne thaw bun ki jmgshitom, 
jingwit jingkulmar-jingmut, jingkylla-dur, kylla-sur jong kiba 
bun bah ki ktien bad ki kyntien, haduh ba lah ban don ki 
kyntien kiba ym lah tip janai shuh la ka tynrai ne ka tyllong 
jong ki Lada jia ba dei ban da thoh bniah, tang kane ka 
Pakhet-ktien kan dawa kawei ka lynnong kaba jrong Te hynrei 
tang kat ban shu pashat jingmut, yn shu jer iwei-ar tang kum 
ban ai nuksa Ki rukom pakhet ki don bun jait, kaba mait, 
sloit, pom, kdiah, ot, dam, pynjah-dak, pynjah-kynnoh, tan, 
ring, thait, khet, thheh, ngor bad bun kiwei pat ki rukom.

Ia kane ka bynta, la dep lalam-jingmut kumba ka paw ha 
ka kot Katto-Katne Shaphang ki Katto-Katne (Ka Thup I)

Don bun ki ktien, ki kyntien bad rukom phawar ne jingong- 
tymmen kiba ha ka jmgpyrshang ban pakhet ne pynlyngkot ia 
ki da kaba shu pom dak, dam, sloit ne da kiwei pat ki rukom 
pakhet, ki la kylla dur noh, teng teng haduh u pud ba la shitom 
ban tip la ka dur tynrai bapura jong ki. Kawei pat, kajujia ia 
uba bun uba lang ba haba ym kham pyndonkam shuh, klet noh 
wat ia kaba la tip .

Don ki bnew kiba na kano-ma-kano ka daw, la ju kham 
tip paidbah la ki da ka kyrteng sin ne khot leit donakor ban ia 
ka kyrteng kumba la jer la thoh ia ki; 1 de hateng-hateng ka 
long synem kumne mo bad ki katto-katne ki ktien.

ka ‘angle’, ka ‘comer’, ka jaka ka puta (locality), ka ‘Dong’ kum 
ka tiar (mould) ne jingthew haba sam-nar,ne ka jingsawa haba 
shoh ne tied la ka kynja nar, etc Na ka ktien ‘Blei’ loh pakhet

‘Khla’, ‘la-man, la-thap-sim, na ka ‘Smang’ loh mang-kwang,

syiem).

Sngap pat kum kane, une u dei u Nongbylla u dei ruh u 
Nong Laban (kata uba ka shnong ka dei ka Laban). Uba kit-

ruh ban ong u Nong-Pymap-nong {hired killer), ne lyngkot shuh-

‘u Nong’.

ong u Sam-at kaba mut la u Nongshohnoh)

kiaw, miaw, mraw, maw’, etc

kiba radbah eh ha ka History ka Thoh ka Pule Khasi, la

kaba skhem na ka bynta ban nang pynroi pynbha la ka ktien 
Khasi ha ki pateng ki ban dang wan.

Khynnah-thang-ktung Katkum kane ka rukom pakhet, 
ka jingmut ka long la ki khynnah ki bym pat nang ban thang 
bha tang la ka ktung ha ka rukom kaba dei. Ka kam ban thang 
ktung kam dei kaba da suk eh Kaba ki mut ban ong ka long 
1 a ki bnew ki bym pat long ‘mature’, ki dei kiba dang shu leh 
rangbah, kim pat dei ki rangbah Haba kren lyngkot ne pakhet 
ki shu ong ‘khynnah thang ktung’

Don ki ktien kiba haba thoh ne kren ki sawa kum ka 
plural, hynrei ha ka jingmut pat ki long singular Kum ki 
syiem, ki bei ki pa, ki blei Katha ong ki syiem (ka mut la u 
syiem)

Ka rukom pyndonkam la ka ktien ‘loi’ Kum u ai tang ka 
ja loi, ne ka sha loi, kaba mut ba u ai tang ka ja suda ne ka 
sha suda U don ne u sah marwei-loi, kaba mut ba u don ne u 
sah tang ma u marwei Ka mut ruh ba ym tam ne duna shuh, 
kum ‘saw-ar-shyieng loi’, ka mut thik katta

Don ki ktien kiba haba ban arsien 1 a ka juh sngew kumba 
shngam, ne poi bha poi belt, hynrei lada ong pat tang shisien 
ong, kam da sawa sngewtynnat, kum ha ka jmg ong, Lada ym 
‘lei-lei’ te bha, kaba ma ka long ba loh ‘lei’ Nga lei, ngam tip. 
Nga te da lei-lei ruh ngam kohnguh

Lynnong II

KATTO-KATNE KI JINGPASHAT 
JINGMUT SHAPHANG KA RUKOM 
PYNDONKAM IA KA KTIEN ‘KHUBLEI’ 
BAD IA KI KYNTIEN BA KUT KA 
KYNNOH HA U ‘IA’

Ka Jingmut ka Ktien Khublei

Uwei u paralok uba dei u ‘khar Bengali, u bym nang 
Khasi (lait noh tang shi kyntien bad kata ka dei ka ktien 
‘Khublei’), u la lathuh ba haba u leit thied dohkha na Iewduh, 
u la dait dor la ka dohkha bad 1 nongdie-dohkha iba dei ki 
kynthei Khasi, hynrei un man mynjur satia Khatduh, u ong, 
nga la shu ong la ka ong, ‘Khublei, Khublei, Khublei’, hangta, 
I la phuh sam-rkhie, bad I la mynjur ha kata ka dor ba u la 
pan.

Na kane ka jingjia, u Nongthoh u la shu kren bina noh, 
ba imat lada uno-uno u bnew u la nang 1 a kane ka ktien 
‘Khublei’, u la lah hi ban kam ba u la nang Khasi shikyntien 
ar kyntien namar ba kane kawei ka ktien ka kit la kumno- 
kumno ym duna shiphew tylli ki jingmut (meaning)

Te tang kat ban pynsabut la katei ka nia, u Nongthoh u 
pyrshang ban jer ne kdew katto-katne ki rukom pyndonkam la 
ka ktien ‘Khublei’.

Te shwa ban poi shata, u Nongthoh u la kdew teng shisien 
ha ka jmgpule paper ha Seng Jingtip Hall (1968) ‘ba kiba bun 
ki ktien Khasi ki long ki ktien shi kynnoh’. Halor kane ka juh

8

ka nongnm, kane ka ktien Khu-blei imat ka wan na kine ki 
kyntien 1) Kyr, 2) Khu bad 3) Blei, kata, U Blei un kyrkhu 
ne Yn kyrkhu U Blei ne U Blei Un long lem bad phi (kaba mut, 
lait noh haba kren-mynthi, ka bud ka sngewnguh, ka kyrkhu 
bad ka jmgsuk).

Haba shu peit na sla, imat kane ka ktien ‘kyr’ ka lahap 
sur ne hap kyrdan bad ki ktien kum ‘Kyn’, Tyn’ ‘Pyn’, etc.

Shaphang ka ktien ‘khu* pat, kham i jwat ban lashem 
bad ka kum ka Verb, ne wat lada lashem teng khat ruh, l 
shitom ban kem la ka jingmut ne snur-jingmut Kum ka Noun 
te imat ka mut la u dkhotmet, kata, “u Khu”. Ngi shem ruh 
la ki ktien ‘at-khu’ ban mut at-nud, sat-nud, wit, thut ne bym 
suk ki jmgsngew-mynsiem (mood) ba piaw ne phoi khleh bishni 
bad kiba kum kita ki jingmut. Shuh-shuh, ngi lahem ruh haba 
ka la tabah bad kiwei pat ki ktien kum

Khu-lom

Khu-snam

Khu-n

Khu-tia

Khu-bor

Khu-ti

Khu-re

Khu-slai

(Wooden Sandals)

Khusnam (Honour)

Khun

Khutia

Khubor

Khuti

Khure

Khuslai etc

la ka ktien ‘Khusnam’ ruh, niar eh ban lashem bad ka 
kumka Verb, lehse tohkhat eh ngi lashem kum ha kane - ki 
Khun-Kha ki la lawan nam wan Khu-snam la la ka Mei-kha, 
ki da ong ruh, ‘to khie leit suk...”

Ka jingthmu jong kane ka jmgthoh ka long tang kat ban 
shu pashat wai-dong ha ki nongpule kiba shai khmat ba kin 
lawad-iathud, la phana, la buh, la bate kyrdan katba hap u 
sawar uba thew uba woh la ki ktien.

Ka Biria shaphang ka ktien M +

Ka ktien, ba la buh kyrdan kum ka language no vvat tang 
kum ka dialect, ka don hi la kiei-kiei ki jinglong khyllah kiba 
maian kiba lapher kawei na kawei pat ka jait ktien syriem hi 
Rumba lapher kawei ka jaitbynriew ne kynhun-bynnew na kawei 
pat Ki don ki jait ktien kiba pahuli dak-thoh , kumta ba ki lah 
ban thoh la kiba bun ki pngpynmih jingsawa Don jiat ki jait 
ktien kiba kham duk dak-thoh ne don tang kham khyndiat 
tylli, pynban ki pyndonkam ne pynleitsiew da ujuh u dak ban 
mut na ka bynta bun tylli ki jait jingsawa, synem kumba leh 
ha ka ktien Phareng Don teng-teng ba ki da pynkup-pynnam 
pher la ujuh u dak, ban pynbiang 1 a ki jingdawa da kaba 
pynspong,j>yntap-tupia, pynphong-pansngiat, pyiunih-reng bad 
kum kita ki rukom Shi wat, la kham slem, la don hi ka 
khlieng ka tyng-ab kaba pasiaw ha ki tyndongskhor u nongthoh, 
ba ka ktien Arabic, Persian bad Sanskrit ki long hapdeng kiba 
kham janai, nangta pat hana ba ka ktien Persian bad ka ktien 
French ki long hapdeng kiba sawa-sngewthiang, bad ki lalap ba 
kaba jwat ne shim por eh ban nang dei ka ktien China (haba 
shu ong kyllum) ( Chinese language ).

nangta ki 1 lynruh-lynram (clumsy). Hynrei mynta sngewmat

U ym leit = Um leit

U yn ym leit = U’n ’m leit 9

ne un ym leit ne u nym leit

Nga yn ym leit = Ngan ym leit

ne nga nym leit 9 = Ngam leit.

Ngam kam ne dawa ba nga tip la kiwei pat ki jait ktien, 
kat ban ai jmgkynthoh halor ka rukom spel dak. Hynrei tang 
kat na kaei kaba nga lah synei ban sngewthuh malu-mala nga 
shu pashat jingmut halor kane ka phang. Kum u Dkhot jong 
ka Assam Civil Service, la dawa la ngi ba dei ban tip katto- 
katne shaphang ka ktien Assamese, Garo, etc tang kat ban lah 
ban pyniaid la ki kam ki jam ha ka por ka jingsynshar ka 
sorkar Assam

Dang nyngkong sngewphylla ban shem ba ki don bun tylli 
ki dak-thoh Haba shu kren kyllum, ki bun ki ‘d\ ki ‘N’, ki ‘S’, 
ki T bad kiwei kiwei de ki dak thoh Kumno ba ki pyndonkam 
da uwei u jait ‘T’ ha kawei ka ktien, bad da uwei pat u jait ha 
kawei pat Kumta ki spel dak katba kumba long ka rukom 
kynnoh la ki ktien kiba lapher Tang kat ban shu loh jingmut, 
ki don ki dak-thoh 1a u ‘O’, ‘a’, Y (ar jait) u ‘u’ (ar jait) u ‘n’, 
u ‘E’, u ‘ui’, ‘ew’ ‘O’ ‘ong’ ‘ah’, sa u ‘ko’ ‘kho’, ‘go’, ‘gho’, ‘ngo’, 
‘so’, ‘sho’, ‘ja’, ‘jha’, ‘Nio’, nangta To’, ‘Tho’, ‘Do’, ‘Dho’, ‘No’, 
‘Po’, ‘Pho’, ‘Bo’, ‘Bho’, ‘Mo’, ‘Jo’, ‘ro’, ‘lo’, ‘wo’, ‘ho’, bad kiwei- 
kiwei de Shisien ong ki don ar jait ki ‘N’, ar jait ki "F nalor 
bun kiwei pat kiba kum kme Ha ka ktien Khasi, ngi sngew 
shitom, namar hap spel ‘dom’ haba u briew u dom lano-iano, 
bad spel biang ‘dom’ haba mut la ka ksew kaba ngi ong Ka Dom 
(kynnoh Dome) ka ksew beh mrad, kum ha ka sentens ‘Beh 
khun ka Dom’ (Dome)

Kumba nga la ong ha kaba sdang, kane ka phang ka 
dawa 1a ka jmgtai-bmah bad tai-phah da kito kiba la loh

19

jingpyntbit ha kane ka bynta Baroh shi katta, ngi shu iarieh 
tngen hapoh ka trab ‘Para Khasi’ te la 1a sngewthuh hi lada 
pule ne thoh ne kren Khasi, hynrei lada pule da ki bymdei 
Khasi, te bunsien ka mih jmgeh Kum ka nuksa, haba dang 
khynnah nt, ngi kynnoh u ‘me-tiaw’ la ka ktien u ‘met-iaw’, 
ngi da pyrkhat ba ka dei ka kyrteng briew, kumba don ki 
khynnah kiba kynnoh ‘khie-lapeh’ la ka ‘khie lap-eh’ (kme ki 
long tang ki nuksa)

Ka jmgmlien ne laipyndonkam (ka usage) te ka trei don 
bor bha, teng-teng haduh la kaba lait ruh ym kynmaw shuh 
namar ba la mlien Shuh-shuh, sngew ba hateng-hateng ka 
jmgthait, ka jingalhia ruh ka trei, haba dei ban sdang thymmai 
namar hap ban pynshitom Ha ka Longbnew te bunsien hi, ka 
belt ban laid na ba kham jem kham suk (sa shu ai daw pat 
katba sngewbit tang ban pynksan la la ba mut)

Don ka ‘poem’ kaba sawa thawlir kaba la paw ha ki jmgshon 
kiba kham hadien ka kot Duitarn Ksinr Ka kyrteng kane ka 
poetry ka long So Kher So Daloi La pynkynmaw hangne 
khnang ba ki Research Scholar kin nang lathud-iawad Na ka 
daw ba u Nongthoh um lah ban kern lut la ki kyntien kiba don 
ha kane ka poem, kumta la shu kyllum hapoh ki ktien thawlir 
La buh khnang ba kiba kham tip kin sa ai jingbatai kaba 
kham bmah Katba lah synei ban shemphang, imat ka dei ka 
kynja jingrwai phawar kyn-oi-khun-kyn-oi-thiah kaba na ki thain 
synteng Ka jingdon ki kyntien So (saw) kher So (Saw) Doloi So 
(Saw) dong, bad wh u lathuhkhana (tang kat ban sah 
lathuhkhana) ki sawa kumba ki dei na shaphang synteng La 
don teng ki tymmen kiba khana ba shaphang Jamtiapur la ju 
don ka syiem (Rani) ne ki syiem kiba ju n ia ki mrad kum la 
u khla ha ka rukom kumba n 1a ki miaw ki ksew Lehse ka 
ktien ‘Ram Sanchor sanchor ka lah ban lasnoh bad ka syiem 
vnthei jong kito ki hima Kawei pat, ka long kaba sngewbha 
cmii tip ba ha ka literature Khasi, la don ki phawar kyn-oi khun 
naduh hyndai.

Ki ktien-kylla • Kumba la thoh teng shakhmat, la don 
ka por kaba la don kiba tip ne sin 1a u Babu So So Tham kum 
‘U Siej-boh’, bad kata ka la pynlyngngoh la kiba bun Dei haduh

20

ynda la loh jmgpynshai ba hyndai (ha ka por khynnah u Babu 
So So) la uno-uno u kynja newpyrkhat (Philosopher) ki ju sin 
la uba kum uta da u ‘Soh-biej’ bad haba kylla-ktien pat ka long 
‘Siej-boh’

Ka rukom kynnoh ba lapher la ki juh ki vowel kat kum 
ki sentence ba iapher t kum (l)u tang shu shong uh-uh (ka 
motor car) u prie uh-uh Nalor ka duk, ka ban sa ka pang. 
Hynnin ka sngi ka twa noh ka 6a/i-um (2) U ong ba un leit 
shim dawai , ynda ivai ka jmgiaseng Namar ba u la shah bad 
(rejected) kumta um loh rung shuh Namar ba u la shah bat 
(ba don ba bat da ka kti) kumta um lah rung shuh. Mano ban 
tem 1 a ka dmg Uei u ban tern duitara U bud nadien jong u 
tem-shi-tem

Kane ka jmgthoh lehse kan long lymbiang lada ym pashat 
jmgmut shi kyntien ar kyntien halor ka rukom spel dak, 
khamtam eh ha ka jingiadei bad ka jingiapurnia shaphang 
kiwei pat ki jait ktien kum ka ktien Assamese bad Bengali, kiba 
don kajmgiadei kaba la slem Ngam don jmgmut ne kajingtbit 
ban taj ei-ei halor kiwei pat ki jait ktien, hynrei nga shu phalang 
jmgmut da ka jingkyrmen ba ki Research Scholar kin sa tai 
pliah hadien habud da kaba 1 m jmgmut, ba katno ngi, ngi la 
ioh myntoi na ki Longshuwa kiba tip la kiwei de ki jait ktien.

Ngi kitram ka bym lah siewdor shuh ha ki Longshuwa, 
ki nongmait-shrah, ki nongprat lynti la ka thoh ka pule Khasi 
naduh ki juk u William Carey, Krishna Chandra Pal, Alexander 
B Lish kiba la pyrshang ban kylla Khasi la ki katto-katne ki 
bynta na ka Baible ha ka ktien Khasi la ka dang long lyngba 
ki dak Bengali, ban kham kyrpang la u Sahep Thomas Jones 
I uba la kylla Khasi la ki bynta na ka Baible lyngba ki dak 
Roman, bad kumjuli ruh la u Rev W Pryse ha ka kot bapawnam 
jong u, An Introduction to the Khasia Language , 1855 Une u 
Sahep u la ai ha ngi 20 tylli ki dak Roman, 13 tylli dang-siang 
( consonants ) bad 7 tylli ki dang-jur (vowels) La ha nga hi, nga 
kham sngewbha ban ong ki ‘dak-sawa-lem’ la ki consonants bad 
ki ‘dak sawa-hi* la ki vowels U Sahep Pryse u ai hynniew tylli 
ki vowels - a,e,i,o,u,w bad y U Sahep W Pryse u lathuh ba ha 
ktien Bengali don 34 tylli ki consonants bad 16 tylli ki vowels.

21

Kumba la thoh shakhmat, ngi sngewnguh la ki 
jingpyrshang ki Longshuwa kiba la kynthup la u V bad u ‘n\ 
1a u ‘G’ kum u ‘Egg* bad 1a u ‘h’ u ‘Ng’ bad u y kiba lapher 
ka rukom pynmih jingsawa katkum ka jaka ba ki shong haba 
sdang ne haba kut ka ktien ne ka jmgkynnoh

Kumba la lalam jingmut shakhmat, ha ka jingiadei ka 
ktien Khasi bad kiwei pat ki jait ktien, la shem ba ha ki por 
hyndai ki sahep ha ki jingthoh kiba nm, ki pyndonkam beit 
tang da u ‘C’ ha ka jaka u ‘K’ Ki tymmen Khasi pat ki shitom 
ban kynnoh la ki katto-katne ki dak Phareng, tang kum ka 
nuksa shim la u dak ‘G’ ne ‘Go’ ha ka Dkhar, ki kynnoh kum 
u ‘K’ (kumba la batai shakhmat)

Ki don ki dak ba ki pyntawah ne pyntabah ne krap lang 
{conjoin) khnang ba kin lah ban spel dak thik kumba long ka 
jingsawa Ka rukom pynshong kyntien (syntax) ki lapher, ngi 
ong Ngan leit sha lew, ha ka dkhai ki pynshong, Nga lew sha 
leit, ngi ong u para jong nga, ha ka dkhar ka long, jongnga u 
para Namar ba ngi duna dak, da ujuh ngi pyntrei na ka bynta 
bun ki jingsawa bad ki da shim pngiarap bad ki kynja shin 
(signs) ban pym la kata Hadien ba nga la durut. bad ksaid 
shitom katba lah syn-ei ban kem ha ka Khasi na ka jmgthoh 
(Ka Jingdwai u Trai) kumba la ohou ha ka kitab u Rev W 
Pryse kaba la shon ha ki dak Bengali bad luh ba la buh de ha 
ki dak Roman Ngan pyrshung ban buh ha ka Khasi kumne 
khnang ba kan larap la ki scholar kiba pule da hi dak Bengali

22

Ka rukom kynthup, buh thup, buh lynnong, buh 
hara, buh ryqjup katkum ba long ka jingsawa ki ktien 
bad ki snap-ktien Katba lah ban sngewthuh malu-mala, 
imat la ki dak-thoh la kynthup ne buh thup, lynnong ne hara 
(group) ne buh rynjup (cluster) ha ka nongrim jong ka jaka na 
kaba la pynnnh jingsawa Ha kane ka bynta lehse kan larap 
ban nang pynnewspah la ka ktien Khasi, lada lah ban buh ne 
kynthup, kum ki ktien ktang-lyer, ktang-kro, doh-dah (doh- 
dah-jem, doh-dah-eh) (soft and hard palate) Imein (ka bynta ba 
shaneng ki bniat, rmiang ne rynnang, ktien sawa-khmut Lada 
long ban ai kyrteng noh la u thylhej, kum khmut-thylhej, 
ryndang-thylhej, met-thylliej, trai-thylhej, snieh-poh thylliej, etc

Kawei ka phang kaba nga la thap ban klet ka long ba 
hyndai, mar eh ban don ha ka ktien Khasi ka ban kut ka 
jingkynnoh ha u ‘S’ Nga kynmaw haduh ki por ba loh 
Independence, I Babu Rayland Lyngdoh iba long uwei na ki 
nonghikai ha skul Shillong Government High School 1 lathuh la 
ngi ba lehse dei tang ka ktien Mils’ ka kut ha u ‘S’ ha ka ktien 
Khasi kum, u nam hi jhs Mynta-mynne lehse la nang lah ban 
nuh shuh-shuli sa kiwei pat ki ktien ne ki kyntien kiba kut ha 
u ‘S’ kum phus, phos etc Kumba la thoh teng shakhmat, 1 
Babu Rayland 1 la lathuh ba la don teng uwei u Sahep uba wan 
poi sha Ri Khasi, bad u la mroh 1 a ka jingnewspah ka ktien 
Khasi U ong, “Ba kano-kano ka ktien kaba la lah ban don ha 
la ka ktien m ka Bible baroh kawei , kata ka dei ka jait ktien 
kaba newbpah ” Ngi 1 a sngew bina ruh haba la lathuh la ngi 
ba ha kata ka juk, ki newstad ki batai ba ka met u bnew, ka 
dor jong ka ynda la pynshongdor nadong-shadong, ka long tang 
kumba shi tyngka bad khat-ar anna ne Re 1 75 N P da ka dor 
ka pisa ha ki snem 1947-48

Ka rukom pyndonkam ia ka ktien ‘Me’ Ha uba bun 
balang, ne haba shu ong kyllum, kham bunsien ki kynnoh 
kumjuh la u ‘E’ ha ka ktien ‘Me’ lada dei ia u briew ne u Blei 
ha ki kot ki bym ladei bad ki kam Balang, hynrei haba dei pat 
bad ka Baible, bunsien ki kynnoh kham eh la u E ha ka ‘Me* 
haba dei shaphang u bnew, bad kham jem haba mut la u Blei 
Ka rukom pyndonkam ia u T bad u ‘D’ ha ki por hyndai. Kum 
ha ka Jingrwai -

“Ha u Mawsiang ban tei 
Mynsiem jong nga wad eh” ?

Tip ka mut ‘wat’ ne ka mut ‘wad’, namar hyndai ki spel 
la jan kumjuh-kumjuh da u V bad u ‘d’ Ha kane ka jmgrwai, 
ynda la pule baroh ki dkhot, la kham sngewdei ban ong 'wad 
bad ymdei ‘ wat'

loh jaka haba spel dak, hynrei ki ong pat khlem jingsawa 
(silent) kumba long u ‘h’ ha ka ktien Phareng Ka jingmhen

Baible, ki thoh Isaiah, Jeremaiah, Esther, hynrei la u ‘h* ngim 
kynnoh jingsawa, kumjuh ruh haba ki spel ‘Khnstan’, ynda

2 U Por u long u Patsha ka pyrthei U nongthoh u pynshlur

3 Ka thwei kaba jylliew jong ka tlong pyrkhat, ka dei, “Ka

Namar, ka mg, ka kur, ka jait

Un khie u sum, ka stieh ka wait,

Shynrang kynthei ki trei ki beng

Te mar ka kam ka ban sah teng”.

Une u Radbah, u la pynsahnam ruh 1 a ki Khlur kiba la

Thei Khasi kiba la rtap bad u Babu So So Tham

U la sei bad pynkynmaw ia ki lar ki morti, ki mom ki

kan leit la la ka kup ka sem ka nam ka phong, bad ban ym 
shu tynneng, meng bad sarong tang ha ka riam-iapan

KI PHALANG BAD KI PRUID

Ki Phalang bad Ki Pruid Ki don ki jingthoh kiba sngew 
shitom jmgmut hangno ban ai jaka 1a ki ne kumno, ne ha kano 
ka rukom yn buh kyrdan (.classify) la ki Te hynrei ha ka juh 
ka por, ngim sngew kwah ba kiba kum kine km shu jah noh

khnang ba kin ym jah noh ne syntuid noh na ka jmgmut- 
jingpyrkhat, nga la sngewdei ban jer la ki, “Ki Phalang”, bad 
khnang ba km ym jah noh na ka thoh ka tar, ka pule puthi, 
la sngewdei ban jer 1a ki, “Ki Pruid” Kumta, ha kane ka 
jmgthoh, phm shem ba don bun bah ki phalang bad ki pruid 
lehse lah ban ong ha ka phareng “Reflections” and “Scribbles”. 
Ki don bun ki ‘pruid’ bad ki ‘phalang* kiba long lymboit-lymbiang, 
kiba dang dawa shibun ban sain-dur bad shna paka 1a ki bad 
buh 1a ki ha ka jaka kaba lahap la ki

ki jait ktien da kaba pyndonkam kum ka Kur-ktien, ka Kpoh-

Kumba la thoh teng, ngi kitram ha kito baroh kiba la 
pynkhem la ki shrah ka ktien Khasi, ki la don bun ki nur kiba 
la lur bad ngi ngeit kin sa nang sei soh katba nangiaid ki por, 
hynrei ki bynta kiba dang sah shrah ruh ki dang byllai, bad ngi 
kyrmen ba ki Scholar kin nang pynheh pyniar bad kyntiew 
kyrdan la ka ktien Khasi na ka por sha ka por

La ym lah ban da niewtang, hynrei nga pynpaw hangne 
la ka jingsngewnguh bad jingsngewkitram la kito ki Rangbah 
Khasi bad kiwei-kiwei de ki nongthoh kiba la pyrshang shitom 
kumno ban pynbha, pynjanai bad pynnew spah la ka thoh ka 
pule Khasi ( Khasi literature ), bad nga da ngeit skhem ba kin 
nang lamih pateng-pateng ki ban 1a bteng 1a kane ka kam 
katba ki sngi ki nang laid Tang kawei, to ngm ym klet noh ta 
kito kiba la mait la ka phang, kiba la prat la ka shrah bad thorn 
lyngba ki rai-eh-raidam ban pynioh ha ngi ta ki-dak thoh bad 
hikai ruh ta ngi ban thoh ban pule ha la ka jong ka Ktien Lada 
ngi klet la kine ne kren haram-m ‘Jahare’ kumba ju ong ki 
tymmen Khasi, “shisien ka sngi, U Blei un takor 1a ngi ” Hateng- 
hateng kajia ba ynda haba ki khynnah skul ki la kiew sha ki 
kliar ka jingnang-jingstad, ki klet noh 1a kito kiba la btin la

48

hikai la ki ha ki skul puna bad ki per-sara, bad don kiba smat 
ban lalap tang la ki jmgduna kiba kito ki nonghikai ki lah ban 
don Haba ngi dang khynnah, ngi ju sngewbina bad sngewsam- 
mut ba ki Sahep ki kynnoh ‘skem’ la kaba ngi kynnoh ‘skhem’ 
Ka shim por ban sngewthuh ba ha ka ktien jong ki u ‘h’ u dei 
u ‘jak* ( silent ) bad kumta mh, ka dei la ngi ban pyrkhat 
sngewlem bad ban sngewnguh la kaba katno kaba ki la lah, bad 
ym eh la ki jmgduna kiba lah ban don hangne-hangtai. Nga 
kynmaw ba 1 Babu Wilson Reade ha ka por ba 1 hikai skul la 
ngi (shuwa ban loh Independence), 1 ju lathuhkhana 1 ong, w ha 
ka por khynnah jong ngi, ngim ju kynnoh 1a kine ki dak, 
kumba hikai la phi mynta”, kata u dak U, W bad Y

Nga sngewkmen pakai bad kito kiba la buh ha ka Khasi 
ban tip 1a ki ‘vowel* kum ki dak-sawahi bad la ki ‘consonant* 
pat kum ki dak-sawalem Kane lehse kan larap 1a ngi ba yn ym 
shu sngewhun-sngewpura ha ka sawa-hi, Jiynrei ban sawa-lem, 
namar tang ki consonant suda lehse tl kan lymbiang ban kynnoh, 
bad tang ki vowel suda pat kan jlep ka shyoh ”

Ka ktien ka kylla na ka por sha ka por, kumta to ngm la 
laid ryngkat bad ki jingkylla, ngm la pyrshang ban nang kham 
pynbha, pynpliah, pynjanai na ka por sha ka por Ka jingim 
(kumba nga la tlioh teng hawei) ka long tang ka juigpyr shang 
Namarkata, to ngin m pyrshang bad nang laid shaphrang, 
katba shem lad , katba lah bor bad katba shah por

ka dei kaba kham heh eh bad ka don ruh kham sha trai bad 
ka kot salit haduh ka tnum ing jong ka Industrial School, 
tangba kam shym pynjulor ei-ei da ka jinghap jong ka

Naduh ki pateng kiba la leit noh, ki Khasi ki ju sngewbha 
ban pynia-synem la u kynja new rangbah ba u long kum u 
Dieng bah, kumta la ju phawar la ju niewtang wat da ki 
nongiam-wai (kata ki kynthei kiba da wer khnang da ka nong 
na ka bynta ban 1 am bnew haba lap bnew) ba uta uta, u long 
kum u Diengbah uba la pat noh, bad ki da oh ki da kynnoh 
kumno ba ka shnong ka thaw ka la shong syier shong sirnej 
bad sah-khynnah ynda haba kiba kum kita ki bnew ki la lap 
noh Dei nalor kane ka nongrim bad ka rukom pyrkhat Khasi 
ba u babu So So Tham u la thoh la kane ka poetry kumba la 
batai

khyllem katei ka tnat-bah, la pom noh la u Dieng Bilat High

Phareng, ki sa ong kaba sah-kura ne dung-kura kaba mut 
ban sah ne ban la ‘box’ kumba laleh ki shipai Gora, bad 
kham hadien-hadien ki sa shu ong lyngkot noh kaba ‘kura’

shnong

ban jer Ingkhong-Shyllang-mat la u bnew uba la leh la 
kano kano tang ban bit, ban myntoi, ban phoida tang m 1 
met la ]ong khlem salia, khlem khuslai, klilem pyrkhat la

Khie-lap-eh' Ka ktien lap nalor kiwei-kiwei pat ki jmgmut, 
kum ka adverb ka mut shuwa ka por, u lap-lap (he died

ka por ymdei ‘khie-lapeh’ hynrei khie-lap-eh Kaba khie lap 
ka mut ba long stet shuwa la ka por, leh lap ka mut leh 
stet eh leh kyrkieh Ka mut ruh kumba ong ha ka phareng 
‘prematurely’ lap lap ( die prematurely) Don kiba kynnoh 
khie-lapeh ha ka jaka khie-lap-eh Nalor kaba la batai la ka 
khie-lap-eh, ki ong ruh ‘ lap-lap ’ kata haba u briew u lap 
shuwa ban poi sha ka jinglongtymmen, ne kum ba ki ju 
ong, u lap khlem pat dei por

Khlam: Ka khlam-ka-ngoh Ki tymmen ki ong ka khlam la 
kano kano ka jmgpang kaba ktah ne sar ne pymap bun ki 
bnew % ka jmgpang kaba la khie ha ka dur ka epidemic 
Ki tymmen ki kynnoh ka khlam ka ngoh, kata ba ka long 
kaei-kaei ka bym jujia koit barabor, hynrei ka wan ‘Jngoh’ 
ne ‘Ngoh’ teng-teng Kawei na ki jaid jmgpang kaba ju khie 
ha kane ka dui ka long kata kaba ki khot ka pang-iap-them 
lane ka kynja cholera La jer kyrteng kumta namar ki 
tymmen ki ong ba kharn bunsien ki briew ki ju kham loh 
kem 1 a kane ka jmgpang ha ki jaka them, jaka thor ne Ri- 
them Ha ka khlambah (lyngba ka pang-iap-them) kaba la 
khie ha Laban (Shillong) ha ka snem 1879, la ong ba la lap 
144 ngut tang ha shnong Mawkhar la kiba lap ki pynkit 
la ki halor ki kali-bordi, ki tap da ki jam lieh bad leit rah 
sha rud-nong shaphang Mawlai Katta ka trei ka 
jingsheptieng la ki bnew ba hapdeng kata ka jinglyngwit, 
la don teng ba wat la kiba ym pat dkut mynsiem ruh la rah 
bad pynkit lang la ki bad kiba lap, hynrei sngewbina, ba 
wei ‘bym pat lut hi la u khaw’, don hi uba lait 1 m lang kat 
ban sah lathuhkhana Ki tymmen Khasi, khamtam ha ki 
por kiba dang shitom ban loh jmgsumar kumba long ha ki 
por mynta, ki don ka jingpyrkhat tyndi-tynda ba ka long 
kaei-kaei kaba sngewsangsot sngewngiew ruh ban lap da ka 
khlam, namar ba bunsien kim lah ne ym shah lynti satia 
ban da pynleit mam ne loh rukom thinkna Lehse, kane ka 
lah ban long kawei na ki daw ba sa mih ka kynja ktien-tim 
% ‘U ban da shoh ka khlam’ lane ‘Ka ban da shoh ka ngoh’ 
ne haba kren shai, U khun ka pap, U ban da shoh ka ngoh 
Ha ka pyrkhat Khasi, mar eh ban don kaba km sngewbuh- 
khoh ne mew palat nalor kata kaba haba u bnew u lap, 
pynban ym lah satia ban pynleit mam Ki tymmen, la uno- 
uno uba lap khlem pynleit mam shuh tdot, ki ju ong % ‘ Uta 
u la ryngkat ka mracf

Khyrim Ka thew ia ka hima ‘Khyrim’ Kane ka ktien ka lah 
ban don jingiasnoh bad ka ktien ‘Khairam’ jong ki syiem 
Ahom, kumjuh kumba ka ktien Lyngskor ka lah ban don 
jingiadei bad ka ktien ‘Laskar' jong ki Dkhar 
Ki Ain bad ki Adong Katba lah ban tip, ki Khasi ki bud 
pyrkhing bha la ki am bad ki adong shnong na ka bynta ka 
bitlang bad ka bha-lang jong u shnong u thaw Niar eh ban 
don u ban tam-sneng ne pynlah ia kaba ki la rai lang Dei 
na kane ka daw ba ha ki juk kiba khani ha shuwa la ju 
pyndonkam kynrei ia ka ktien, “Jong-shnong” kum “Shoh- 
Jong-shnong” (kaba mut shoh doh ne shoh dohkha), ‘beh- 
jong-shnong' (kaba mut ban beh mrad da ka shnong), shna 
jong-shnong, khang shnong, ap-shnong, leng-da-ka-shnong, 
khytdop shnong, ka urn tong shnong, ka madan shnong, ka 
skill shnong, ka khlaw shnong (lada ka khlaw kyntang ne 
khlaw adong) bad kiwei-kiwoi Shisien ong, la jan ha bun 
bah kici-kiei, hi kham sngewbha ban wag nongrim ne ban 
talyuuur in ka pxrkhat pyrdarh ha ka nongrim jong ka 
shnong , vvat ban pynithuh lalade, bun ki kham sngewbha 
hi ban ong, Nga dei u Nong kaba mut ban kdew 1a ka 
shnong Kane kam ia snoh pat bad ka ktien 1 Nong 9 kaba u 
bnew u kdew ia ka kam no ka jingtrei kaba u trei ne kaba 
un bainong ne sbai-nong, kum Nongtrei, Nongkhan, Nong- 
bylla, Nongknia bad kumta ter-ter

4 Anchor ’

ka rukom ban da kren da khana shaphang uba lap). Katba lah

naduh ba phi la songkha ma phi, ym ju don ka sang ym ju don 
ka ma, ym ju don ka laid lyndet ka leh lyndet, ym ju don ka 
ryngkang, ym ju don ka palat Ka kam ka duk, ka kamai 
kajih, ka lynti ka dwar ki roi ki pa bad ki laid beit laid ryntih, 
ym ju don ka thok ka shukor, ka raibi ka sangbi ne ka tyrut 
ka smer, baroh ki laid belt da kaba suk kaba bha suda, te 
kumta kein ko Kynrad, kumba phi la kamai shitom, hynrei wei 
la dkut hi la u ksai hukum U Nongbuh-Nongthaw, te to khie 
da leit suk leit biang sha la ka kmie ka kiaw wat leit 
sahnud, wat buhnud, wat leit buh-khoh buh-jaw Kumba phi la 
lohi hi ba ngi la la nam, la wan khusnam ma ngi la phi, u kur 
u kha ryngkat ki lok ki jor, to da kyrkhu ma phi la ki kur ki 
kha, na dwar U Blei (ym dei nawei pat) To kyrkhu ruh ba kin 
long-bnew ki khun ki kti ki pyrsa ki ling To ka jmgiap kan 
ym wan tyrut kan ym hiar rai-bi (kata ba kan ym wan khah- 
khah ne long tyrut) wei phi la dei ban leit noh sha kawei ka 
pyrthei, wat leit sah nud shuh, wat leit wan khnng shuh, leit 
biang sha la ka kmie ka kiaw

Ki tymmen ki ngeit ruh ba nalor kaba pyndep-pynbiang la 
ki mam ki rukom na shabar, donkam ruh ban leh da ka 
mynsiem kaba sngur kaba nylla bad ha ki ktien don-akor

donburom haba ki kren Lada bakla na kane, khlem artatien

ka kit la katei kajuh ka jingmut ha ka jmgiasnoh bad ka

Soh-syrtet Ka jait-dieng kaba ki tymmen ki ong kaba lah bha 
ban shan la ka ding Don ruh kiba kynnoh ka ‘dieng-tait-

pynthohdak pyrshah la ki Hyndai ka ju don ka 
diengsohsyrtet ha ka lynti ban leit sha Iewbah Sohra, bad 
ka long ka dieng kaba bit bha ban rieh tngen na ka sngi 
bad rieh slap haba slap ki briew kiba rieh tngen hapoh 
hangta ki thoh shun bad leh katba ki mon

Sop Mynshuwa eh ki pyndonkam la kane ka ktien ban mut 
kaba sop (cover) kum sop-khniang, sop-khnam, sop-wait, 
etc Ki ong ruh sop-kti, sop-kjat, sop-lyeit, sop-ti-poh, sop-ti-

syaid, sopti-leit-bylla, sopti-pulit, sopti-shipai, etc.

bunsien ki bam lang bad u atta ne u pun.

jingpyrkhat ne ka rngai. Ka lah ban mut la ki dak ki shin 
kiba wan shat wan paw.

Tymmen-kum. Ki don ki jaka ba ki khot kumne ia ki bnew 
ki bym pat loh bor pura, kiba shu iarap pyniaid, pyntyllun 
1a ka shalyntem ne ka kam. Ka long synem kumba ong 
acting. U Syiem-kum, u tymmenbasan-kum, u rangbah- 
shnong-kum, etc. Kane ka long ka rukom kren ki new 
hyndai, katba ym pat don ne loh syiem, uta-uta u nang 
pyniaid ia ka kam kum u syiem.

Tymmen-u-san Uba la san (mature and experienced man). Ki 
ong ruh u Basan Ki kham pyndonkam ha ka lain synshar- 
khadar Ha ka rukom synshar Balang pat ki khot ki 
tymmen-basan ban pyniapher na u Basan ne u Longsan.

Tyng-ier. Don kiba 1a kane ka juh, ki ong ka tympan na kaba 
ngi loh 1a ka ktien koh-tympan, Don pat kiba ong ka ryng- 
ten. Ka long ka phrah ba la sdien halor ka dpei ha kiba ki 
buh, ki thad bun kiei-kiei (a bamboo frame work, suspended 
over the fire place for drying meat, etc.).

Tyngshop ka puron. Hyndai ki ong u tyngshop bad ka puron- 
pyntieng tyngab, ki ju buh ha ki lyngkha-nsai. Mynta ki 
pyndonkam la ka ktien tyngshop ban mut la ki characters 
ha ki jmgiathuh khana ne jmgialehkai drama. La 
pyndonkam ruh ban mut ne leng na ka bynta ka Iawbei 
bad u Thawlang hyndai. Imat tyngshop ka kham thew la 
u shynrang, puron pat la ka kynthei

Tyngshop. Ka dur bnew (lada kynthei ne shynrang) ba la shu 
thaw daei-daei ban I kum u briew (effigy)

Tynjuh khabah Kaba shu kwah ne pyrshang ban loh ia kaei- 
kaei kaba sha ba palat ka bor.

Tyrko- Kaba lalap-ianew, la-pur-ma, iatai kham bmah (discuss 
things m detail)

u anna , kaba mut ba uba biej u don hi la ka jmgduna.

Ka buit-ka bor: Ka ktum ‘ buit ' ka dei kawei na kito ki ktien 
kiba don bun ki ktien kynnoh. Kum ka buit-ka bor, ka buit 
ka mynsiem ka buit ka sap, ka buit-ka akor, Haba shu

lada ka mut da kumwei pat, ki da pynshai kum ka buit-

mut ba ka buit ka long ka bor U la jop ha ka jingiakhun 
ymdei da ka bor, hyniei da ka buit Ka buit-ka sap ka 
kham thew eh la ka akor (behaviour ) Ki ju kynnoh, ka 
buit-ka bur ka dei ban laid ryngkat-ryngkat bad ka akor, 
tipbriev -tipblci, ha ka jmgmut ban ym pyndonkam kulmar 
la ka La ka buit lie la ka bor, ka shong shibun ha u bnew 
kumno u pyndonkam la ki

Ka dak-ka shin Ka dak ka don bun ki jmgmut bad ki rukom

(sign) da kaba yn lah ban pyniapher ne ithuh la kaei-kaei.

Ka dhari-ka baranda: Ka dhat i ka long ka bynta jong ka mg

Ka dorbar-ka sorbar: Dorbar ka long kaba kham pyndonkam

Ka dor-ka mun Ka dor ka mut paidbah bad kyllum, ka mut 
belt la ka dor (price) jong kaei-kaei Ka mur pat ka mut ym 
tang la ka dor, hynrei ka lah ban mut la ka dor ka kuna 
Don ba ki tymmen ki kynnoh dain-mur, synem kumba ong

Ka dukha-ka sukha: Dukha ka kham mut kaba noh-mynsiem, 
kaba jew-mynsiem (disappointed), sukha pat ka kham mut 
la ka sngew-pisa-jmgmut, kaba duh jmgkyrmen (frustrated)

Ka dulan~ka paki: Dulan ka kham mut eh la ki mg ba la

kita Paki ruh ka mut hi 1a ki kynja mgbha-ingmiat ba ju 
shong ki kynja syiem kynja patsha

Ka dur-ka dar: Ka dur bunsien eh ka thew 1a ka dur-khmat, 
namar hiar eh ban don uba shah shondur khlern da paw ka 
khmat Haba ithuh la u bnew ruh ki ong ithuh-khmat Ka 
dar pat kam mut tang la ka khmat, hynrei la kano-kano 
ka bynta jong ka mot Ki ju kynnoh, u la kylla bad dur bad 
dar, kaba mut ba ka jingkylla ka la ktah ymdei shuh tang 
la ka dur-khmat, hynrei ka la ktah ruh la kiwei-kiwei pat 
ki bynta jong ka met

Ka dwar ka lynti: Kane ka ktien dwar ka don ka jingmut 
kaba lar H>ndai ka kham mut la ka jingkhang, ka khvrdop 
ha ka jmgiadci khain tarn bad ki jaka khappud sha n 
dkhar Haba kynnoh lang ka dwar ka lynti ka thew ruh la 
ka khai-ka pateng, ka leit ka wan bad kiwei kiwei de ki 
rukom jingiadei, kum ka ‘ trade and commerce 9 
4 communication \ 4 commercial intercourse ’ etc Ka ktien dwar 
ka don ka jmgmut kaba jylliew namar, ngi loh la ka jingong 
'Barn kwai ha Dwar u Blei*

Ka it-ka hima: Man la ka hima ka don la ka it ( territory ), 
kaba mut ba ka don la u pud u sam (boundary) Don ruh 
ba ki pyndonkam la ka ktien it ban mut 1a ka jinglong ka 
jaka khamtam ha kaba long lum long wah, long at long 
them, kum ka it beh mrad

Ka jait-ka kynja: Kynja ka kham long ha ka jmgmut kaba 
kyllum bad ka bym da thikna, ka bym shai, ki ju kynnoh, 
u kynja mrad, u kynja-khmang, u kynja dawai Khong pat,

166

ki kham ong, ki kham tabah bad uno uno u briew, kat 
kumba long ka jingiadei. Ka ktien sker ka don malu-mala 
ka jingiasynem jingmut bad ka ktien thana-tbaker.

Ka jait-ka sker, ka jait-ka kynja, ka jait-ka khong: Ka jait 
ka kham thew m kaba kham heh, sker la kaba kham rit, 
kum hapoh kawei ka jait, ki don bun ki sker. Ia ka ktien 
sker la pyndonkam ruh ban mut 'Index'. Ka khong pat, 
kumba la kdew, ka kham thew eh la ka jingiadei bad bunsien 
ka laid ryngkat bad ka ktien bynrap ban batai ia kata ka 
jingiadei.

Ka J aka-ka muluk Jaka ka kham mut kyllum. Ka muluk ka 
thew eh ia ka Ri ia ka hima namar haba ki kren shapang 
la u Nongdie ri ( Quisling ) ki ong u Nongsiar-muluk.

Ka Jaka-ka puta Ka jaka ka don ka jingmut kaba kyllum 
bad paidbah Puta pat ka kham mut ia ka jaka ba la 
pynthikna la u pud u sam bad tip ruh ia u Trai-Khyndew. 
Lehse ka lah ban don jingiasnoh bad ka dulir-ai-khyndew 
ba tip kum ka 'Patta'

Ka jynjar-ka trah: Jynjar ka mut kyllum, ka lah ban dei 
kaba khraw ne kaba nt ha la ka jingshem Ki kynnoh ruh, 
jynjar-mynsiem, jynjar-jmgmut nalor kiwei kiwei pat ki 
jait jynjar Trah ne jynjar-trah , ki kham ong eh, ynda haba 
ka jynjar ka la ktah la ka thiah ka dem, ka shongsuk- 
shongshngam

Ka kdun ka kdan: Kdun ka kham thew eh ia kaba long 
lynghung ne khun-lynghung (concave), bad kdan pat ka 
kham mut la kaba long lynghang ne khun-lynghang ( convex )

Ka khet-ka bishna: Ia ka ktien khet kham bunsien ki pyntabah 
lang bad ka ktien syiem, kum ka khet-syiem bad bunsien 
ka kit ia ka jingmut jong ka bor ba uta uba shong halor ka 
khet u bat Bishna pat ka kynthup ruh ia ka jingpymtynnat 
pynwandur ia ka khet da kiei-kiei kiba kordor kum ka 
bmat hati, ki maw hira ki mawkordor abd kiwei-kiwei.

Ka khlam-ka ngoh: Khlam ka mut ia kano kano ka jmgpang 
kaba ktah kaba pymap bun ki bnew. Ka jmgpang kaba la 
sar bun ki mynsiem (epidemic). Ka ngoh, ka jingpang ka 
bym ju kham jia barobor, hynrei kaba wan jngoh teng-teng.

167

Kawei na ki jait jmgpang-khlam ka dei kaba ki tymmen, ki 
ong ka Iap-them (cholera)

Ka khlaw-ka btap: Khlaw ka long ka ktien ba kham 1a 
pyndonkam paidbah, bad bunsien ki da jer kyrteng kum ka 
khlaw-adong, ka khlaw-kyntang ka khlaw-shnong, khlaw- 
ksur, khlaw-lyngdoh etc Btap pat ka thew 1a ka jaka kaba 
long khlaw long sahium, kaba sah ne lehnoh khlem 
pynkhuid.

Ka khor-ka khriam: la baroh ar, ki tymmen Khasi ki ihewkor 
la ki, bad la kinc ki jait jam, ki buh tyngkai bha, bad ki 
pyndonkam tang ha ki lat kiba kyrpang.

Ka khroh-ka kbih: Ka khroh ka long ha ka rukom kaba belt 
bad kyllum Ka kbih ka kham mut eh la ka jingkhroh kaba 
don kaba pyn-ap bad ka kynja pngkular ne jmgthong Ki 
kynnoh, ‘la uta-uta, ymdei shuh tang ba ki khroh, hynrei

sumar pang, ki ju kynnoh, naduh ba u pang, kiba haing- 
hasem ki la wad lut u stad u jhad, hynrei ym pat loh 
jingiarap satia. Ka ktien jhad ne *u jhad ’ ka siat jmgmut 
ia u nongsumar pang uba la long kum u kynja ‘ specialist \ 
uba la lah ban shemphang bad ai jingiarap la kiba bun 
srukom ki jait jingpang-jingshitom, ai kan dei ka pang ne 
ka jha, ka jhai ne ka kjut bad luwei-kiwei

196

U sup-u thiar: U sup u long u kynja shang ba la thaw la 
tham kyrpang na ka bynta ban buh ia kiba bun jait kiei- 
kiei. Ia u sup ki pyndonkam ruh kum ka thiar buh kba. Na 
kata ka daw ki kynnoh u sup-u thiar.

U syiar u shan: Ki tymmen Khasi ki kynnoh u syiar u shart 
namar ba ki ngeit ba u kyrshan ne u shan la ka nia ka 
jutang, u shan ruh la ka dwar ka lynti.

U syiem u kmie: Naduh hyndai ki kynnoh u Syiem u Kmie, 
namar nalor kiba bun syrtap ki kamram kiba u syiem u dei 
ban kit ban pyndep, kawei na kita ka long ba u hap ban 
peit na ka bynta ka kupshihang-ka semshihang, ka lapduh- 
ka laptan ha ka rukom syriem kumba ka kmie ka peit ka 
sumar la la ki khun ki kti Lehse dei ruh halor kaba kum 
kane ka nongnm, bad ki kynnoh, haba don u syiem, u don 
ruh u khun u hajar. Hyndai, ki ju kynnoh ruh u syiem u 
kmie

U synhiang u bynhei: U synhiang , ha ki por hyndai, ka 
kham thew eh 1a u synhiang uba da u sbai da ka pisa, ka 
long ka rukom jingsiew kaba da pynbeit thikna, ka lah ban 
long man u bnai ne man u snem Ka long ka kynja 
‘ subscription ’ Hynrei ha kine ki por mynta, ki la 
pyndonkam ruh ban mut ia kiwei-kiwei pat ki rukom noh- 
synmang nalor jong ka pisa, bad kumta ka mut ne kem la 
ka jingmut ruh kum ka ktien “contribute” U bynhei , hyndai 
ki kham thew eh ia ka jmgai kaba ki bnew ki ai hi da lade 
katba ki sngewbit kat kaba ki lah bor namar ba ki 
sngewthuh ba ki dei ban ai ei-ei Ki ju kynnoh, ki pynnoh 
la u synhiang, ki pynhap la u bynhei. Lehse kan kham 
lasynem jingmut bad ka ktien ‘ donation \ Nalor nangta, ki 
lah ban pynhap kat da kaba ki sngewbit, ym dei eh tang da 
u sbai ne da ka pisa Shuh-shuh, hyndai eh, haba ong 
bynhei ka kham thew la kaba lasnoh bad ka jmgpyndep 
mam pyndep rukom.

U syntiew-u skud: U syntiew ki kynnoh la u jmgthung uba 
phuh-syntiew, don uba da seisoh, don ruh u bym seisoh. Ki 
kynnoh ruh, u soh-u syntiew . U skud pat kam mut thang 
1a uba phuh syntiew, hynrei ka kynthup lang ia u kynja

197

sla-syntiew u kynja tyrkhang uba lwbih, uba ltynnat synem 
kumba itynnat u syntiew

U thiar-u tyllu ne ka thiar-ka tyllu: U thiar ka long ka 
jaka buh kba bad kiba bun ki ing ki don la la ka thiar Ka 
tyllu ka long ka thiar kaba la da shna kyrpang da ki bnew 
kiba la loh bun bha u kba Ha ki por hyndai, kim da 
paidbah eh ki bnew kiba don tyllu. Lehse, lah ruh ban 
pyndonkam la ka ktien tyllu na ka bynta ki ing ba la thaw 
kyrpang ban tyngkai ban kynshew la u khaw u kba ne la 
kiwei pat ki kynja jingbam

U thied u jaw: U thied u long ha ka jingmut kaba lar bad 
kyllum, u lah ban long u thied jong kaei-kaei. Haba ong u 
thied, bunsien ka mut la uba lah ban ithuh, uba lah ban 
sngewthuh haba twad ba tba, ba khem ba jyllud, wat lada 
jia ba um paw shabar ne lah ban lohi Ki don bun rukom 
bun jait ki thied bad ki trei la la ka jmgkam U jaw pat ka 
kham thew la ki jait thied kiba nt kiba ni kynthup ruh la 
kito kiba ym lah shuh ban lohi ne ithuh da ka khmat doh 
ki kynja thied nt ( nerves )

U truh-u asem: Truh ha ka jingmut kaba kham paidbah ka 
kham thew la u krai-truh uba ki tymmen Khasi ki ju 
hiewkor namar ki ong ba u long u jingbam uba lah ban 
kyrshan wat ha ki por kiba tan, ki kynja nem-smew Nangta 
shuh-shuh, don ka rukom jingialehkai maw-truh haba ki la 
lehkai maw-kyntmg Don ruh ka jingpang ba ki ong kaba 
‘sa-niang truh’ la ka ktien ‘ asem ’ te ym da lah ban batai 
thikna, namar lehse ki tymmen ki lah ban mut la u ‘mi- 
sem’ u kynja jyrmi, u kynja jingthung uba pur Ka don ka 
shnong ha ka hima Khynm, kaba tip kum ka khheh-asem

U tymmen-u san, ki kynnoh ruh u tymmen-u kro: U 
tymmen , ka kham thew eh la uno-uno u bnew uba la 
kynjoh sha ka jmglong tymmen, U san pat, ka kham mut 
eh 1a uno-uno uba la san ha ka jmgtip-jingshemphang ha 
kano-kano ka bynta , wat lada jia ba um pat da tymmen ha 
ka rta. U kro pat ka kham mut la uno-uno u bnew uba 
nalor ba u la tymmen hynrei u long u kynja bnew uba tip- 
tymmen la kiei-kiei kiba shongsbai bad kiba lah ban long

198

ka jmgmyntoi ka jingiarap la ki longdien. Baroh ki kro ki 
dei ki tymmen, hynrei baroh ki tymmen kim dei ki kro.

Umshit umpjah: Kane ka ktien ka kit ar tylli ki jingmut. 
Kaba nyngkong ka long ba ki pyndonkam la ka umshit bad 
umpjah na ka bynta ban sumar pang Te ki ong ruh ban 
khem umshit-uwpjah ban pynjem-pynkhiah la kano-kano 
ka jingbym 1a sngewthuh jingmut hapdeng ki ar liang 
mamla Ki ong umstut-umjah ba ki mut 1a ka umshit jong 
ka doh kaba ki la shet syriem kum ka kynja syrwa. Te ki 
kynnoh ‘khem umshit-khem-umjah’, ban wanrah biang la 
ka jingiasuk-iabha hapdeng ki ar hang mamla Kawei ka 
long kumba khem da ka umshit-umjah Kawei pat ka 
jingmut ka long syriem kumba ai ne lashim bynta ha ka 
bam ka sa ban wallam pat la ka jingiabeit-iabha.

Wad-thud: Wad ka kham mut kyllum kuba wad la kaei-kaei. 
Thud ka kham mut ba da wad bmah ban lap ban shem la 
kaei-kaei kaba u bnew u donkam Ka long ka rukom wad 
rukom iwet kaba da pynshitom Ka ktien wad kan kham 
lajan jingmut bad ka 'seek 9 katba thud kan lajan bad ka 
ktien 4 search 1

Wanwir-wanhap: Wanwu ka kham mut eh la uba wan sakma, 
wan poi sakma naoi-re-naei, kumba ki kynnoh ruh u poi- 
wir, u phet-wir, kaba mut kaba poi sakma, phet sakma, 
shuh-shuh ki kynnoh ruh TJ khun-wir-spah’ ban mut u 
khun uba pynwir-pynsakma la ka spah ki kmie ki kpa U 
wanhap ka kham mut ba la hap ban kit ban bah 1a uno- 
uno, lah ban dei uba ladei bynta ne u bymiadei bynta, lah 
ban dei kaba tip lupa ne ka bymtip lupa
